# Duck Delivery planning

Read the complete puzzle before planning. Coordinates are zero-based `[x,y]`, with x increasing right and y increasing down. Terrain and goal positions are fixed. The duck can walk and push exactly one parcel into an empty floor square; it cannot pull parcels or push a chain.

1. Identify each parcel, its candidate goal, and the square the duck must occupy behind it for each push.
2. Plan a route, tracking the duck and every parcel after each push. Do not treat a goal as a wall or remove a delivered parcel from the board.
3. Check walls, other parcels, and the duck's ability to reach the next pushing position. Prefer a short route that leaves room to maneuver.
4. Use the sandbox to check the proposed sequence with the supplied canonical simulator when it is available. Respect the task's simulation and submission limits.
5. Return one JSON object with exactly a `moves` array containing only `up`, `down`, `left`, or `right`. Do not return source code, commentary, or a new level.

If given failure feedback for a second submission, start planning again from the original puzzle. Do not continue from the failed final state. Never claim delivery unless every parcel is on a goal.

'EOF'\n/* Generated from sokoban/engine.ts; sha256=b1c7a7a9593b7ed1aa3c027f6daf99221571115f3d51632899e30ea1f3e4a8ae; do not edit. */\nglobalThis.__DUCK_DELIVERY_ENGINE__ = (() => {\nconst directionDelta = { up: [0, -1], down: [0, 1], left: [-1, 0], right: [1, 0] };\nconst DIRECTIONS = new Set(Object.keys(directionDelta));\nconst key = ([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`;\nconst same = (a, b) => a[0] === b[0] && a[1] === b[1];\nconst occupied = (parcels, p) => parcels.findIndex((parcel) => same(parcel, p));\nfunction validateLevel(level) {\n    const width = level.terrain[0]?.length ?? 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? [...beyond] : [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes + 1 };\n}\nfunction isWon(level, state) { return state.parcels.every((parcel) => level.goals.some((goal) => same(parcel, goal))); }\nfunction serialize(state) { return JSON.stringify({ duck: state.duck, parcels: [...state.parcels].sort((a, b) => key(a).localeCompare(key(b))), moves: state.moves, pushes: state.pushes }); }\nfunction hash(state) { return serialize(state); }\nfunction plan(moves) { validatePlan(moves); return { moves: [...moves] }; }\nfunction validatePlan(moves) {\n    if (!Array.isArray(moves) || moves.length > 100 || moves.some((move) => typeof move !== \"string\" || !DIRECTIONS.has(move)))\n        throw new Error(\"plan must contain at most 100 valid directions\");\n}\nfunction replay(level, moves) {\n    validatePlan(moves);\n    let state = initialState(level);\n    const trace = [serialize(state)];\n    for (const direction of moves) {\n        state = step(level, state, direction);\n        trace.push(serialize(state));\n    }\n    return { trace: Object.freeze([...trace]), finalState: state, attempts: moves.length, won: isWon(level, state) };\n}\nfunction cornerDeadlocks(level, state) {\n    const goalKeys = new Set(level.goals.map(key));\n    const width = level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isWall = (x, y) => y < 0 || y >= level.terrain.length || x < 0 || x >= width || level.terrain[y][x] === \"#\";\n    return state.parcels.filter(([x, y]) => !goalKeys.has(key([x, y])) && ((isWall(x - 1, y) || isWall(x + 1, y)) && (isWall(x, y - 1) || isWall(x, y + 1))));\n}\n  return { directionDelta, validateLevel, initialState, step, isWon, serialize, hash, plan, validatePlan, replay, cornerDeadlocks };\n})();\nEOF\necho \"Engine saved\"\n OUTPUT Engine saved\n\n\t\t\t\t88 button (expanded) Description: Collapse step, Secondary Actions: Collapse\n\t\t\t\t89 text Install quickjs and test the plan\n\t\t\t\t147 text sandbox\n\t\t\t\t148 container\n\t\t\t\t\t149 text exit:  0\n\t\t\t\t\t150 container Sandbox view\n\t\t\t\t\t\t151 checkbox Description: Terminal View, Value: 1\n\t\t\t\t\t\t152 checkbox Description: Code View, Value: 0\n\t\t\t\t153 text COMMAND\n\t\t\t\t154 container\n\t\t\t\t\t155 text $ pip install quickjs -q && echo \"quickjs installed\"\n\t\t\t\t156 text OUTPUT\n\t\t\t\t157 text WARNING: Running pip as the 'root' user can result in broken permissions and conflicting behaviour with the system package manager, possibly rendering your system unusable. 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? [...beyond] : [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes + 1 };\n}\nfunction isWon(level, state) { return state.parcels.every((parcel) => level.goals.some((goal) => same(parcel, goal))); }\nfunction serialize(state) { return JSON.stringify({ duck: state.duck, parcels: [...state.parcels].sort((a, b) => key(a).localeCompare(key(b))), moves: state.moves, pushes: state.pushes }); }\nfunction hash(state) { return serialize(state); }\nfunction plan(moves) { validatePlan(moves); return { moves: [...moves] }; }\nfunction validatePlan(moves) {\n    if (!Array.isArray(moves) || moves.length > 100 || moves.some((move) => typeof move !== \"string\" || !DIRECTIONS.has(move)))\n        throw new Error(\"plan must contain at most 100 valid directions\");\n}\nfunction replay(level, moves) {\n    validatePlan(moves);\n    let state = initialState(level);\n    const trace = [serialize(state)];\n    for (const direction of moves) {\n        state = step(level, state, direction);\n        trace.push(serialize(state));\n    }\n    return { trace: Object.freeze([...trace]), finalState: state, attempts: moves.length, won: isWon(level, state) };\n}\nfunction cornerDeadlocks(level, state) {\n    const goalKeys = new Set(level.goals.map(key));\n    const width = level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isWall = (x, y) => y < 0 || y >= level.terrain.length || x < 0 || x >= width || level.terrain[y][x] === \"#\";\n    return state.parcels.filter(([x, y]) => !goalKeys.has(key([x, y])) && ((isWall(x - 1, y) || isWall(x + 1, y)) && (isWall(x, y - 1) || isWall(x, y + 1))));\n}\n  return { directionDelta, validateLevel, initialState, step, isWon, serialize, hash, plan, validatePlan, replay, cornerDeadlocks };\n})();\nEOF\necho \"Engine saved\" OUTPUT Engine saved\n\n\t\t\t\t102 text Now let me read the duck-planning skill to understand the planning approach:\n\t\t\t\t103 button (expanded) Description: Collapse step, Secondary Actions: Collapse\n\t\t\t\t104 text Read the duck-planning skill documentation\n\t\t\t\t150 text sandbox\n\t\t\t\t151 container\n\t\t\t\t\t152 text exit:  0\n\t\t\t\t\t153 container Sandbox view\n\t\t\t\t\t\t154 checkbox Description: Terminal View, Value: 1\n\t\t\t\t\t\t155 checkbox Description: Code View, Value: 0\n\t\t\t\t156 container\n\t\t\t\t\t157 text COMMAND $ cat /opt/tf/skills/duck-planning/SKILL.md OUTPUT ---\nname: duck-planning\ndescription: Plan legal Sokoban deliveries for Duck Delivery and check parcel pushes before committing a move sequence.\n---\n\n# Duck Delivery planning\n\nRead the complete puzzle before planning. 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? [...beyond] : [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes + 1 };\n}\nfunction isWon(level, state) { return state.parcels.every((parcel) => level.goals.some((goal) => same(parcel, goal))); }\nfunction serialize(state) { return JSON.stringify({ duck: state.duck, parcels: [...state.parcels].sort((a, b) => key(a).localeCompare(key(b))), moves: state.moves, pushes: state.pushes }); }\nfunction hash(state) { return serialize(state); }\nfunction plan(moves) { validatePlan(moves); return { moves: [...moves] }; }\nfunction validatePlan(moves) {\n    if (!Array.isArray(moves) || moves.length > 100 || moves.some((move) => typeof move !== \"string\" || !DIRECTIONS.has(move)))\n        throw new Error(\"plan must contain at most 100 valid directions\");\n}\nfunction replay(level, moves) {\n    validatePlan(moves);\n    let state = initialState(level);\n    const trace = [serialize(state)];\n    for (const direction of moves) {\n        state = step(level, state, direction);\n        trace.push(serialize(state));\n    }\n    return { trace: Object.freeze([...trace]), finalState: state, attempts: moves.length, won: isWon(level, state) };\n}\nfunction cornerDeadlocks(level, state) {\n    const goalKeys = new Set(level.goals.map(key));\n    const width = level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isWall = (x, y) => y < 0 || y >= level.terrain.length || x < 0 || x >= width || level.terrain[y][x] === \"#\";\n    return state.parcels.filter(([x, y]) => !goalKeys.has(key([x, y])) && ((isWall(x - 1, y) || isWall(x + 1, y)) && (isWall(x, y - 1) || isWall(x, y + 1))));\n}\n  return { directionDelta, validateLevel, initialState, step, isWon, serialize, hash, plan, validatePlan, replay, cornerDeadlocks };\n})();\nEOJS\necho \"Engine saved\" OUTPUT Engine saved\n\n\t\t\t\t54 button (expanded) Description: Collapse step, Secondary Actions: Collapse\n\t\t\t\t55 text Save the Python adapter for running simulations\n\t\t\t\t149 text sandbox\n\t\t\t\t150 container\n\t\t\t\t\t151 text exit:  0\n\t\t\t\t\t152 container Sandbox view\n\t\t\t\t\t\t153 checkbox Description: Terminal View, Value: 1\n\t\t\t\t\t\t154 checkbox Description: Code View, Value: 0\n\t\t\t\t155 container\n\t\t\t\t\t156 text COMMAND $ cd /tmp/duck_puzzle && cat > adapter.py << 'EOPY'\n\"\"\"Execute the canonical generated JS in a Python-only TrueForge sandbox.\n\nInstall quickjs in the sandbox, then pass engine.browser.js, level.json, plan.json.\nThis adapter contains no movement rules. 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? [...beyond] : [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes + 1 };\n}\nfunction isWon(level, state) { return state.parcels.every((parcel) => level.goals.some((goal) => same(parcel, goal))); }\nfunction serialize(state) { return JSON.stringify({ duck: state.duck, parcels: [...state.parcels].sort((a, b) => key(a).localeCompare(key(b))), moves: state.moves, pushes: state.pushes }); }\nfunction hash(state) { return serialize(state); }\nfunction plan(moves) { validatePlan(moves); return { moves: [...moves] }; }\nfunction validatePlan(moves) {\n    if (!Array.isArray(moves) || moves.length > 100 || moves.some((move) => typeof move !== \"string\" || !DIRECTIONS.has(move)))\n        throw new Error(\"plan must contain at most 100 valid directions\");\n}\nfunction replay(level, moves) {\n    validatePlan(moves);\n    let state = initialState(level);\n    const trace = [serialize(state)];\n    for (const direction of moves) {\n        state = step(level, state, direction);\n        trace.push(serialize(state));\n    }\n    return { trace: Object.freeze([...trace]), finalState: state, attempts: moves.length, won: isWon(level, state) };\n}\nfunction cornerDeadlocks(level, state) {\n    const goalKeys = new Set(level.goals.map(key));\n    const width = level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isWall = (x, y) => y < 0 || y >= level.terrain.length || x < 0 || x >= width || level.terrain[y][x] === \"#\";\n    return state.parcels.filter(([x, y]) => !goalKeys.has(key([x, y])) && ((isWall(x - 1, y) || isWall(x + 1, y)) && (isWall(x, y - 1) || isWall(x, y + 1))));\n}\n  return { directionDelta, validateLevel, initialState, step, isWon, serialize, hash, plan, validatePlan, replay, cornerDeadlocks };\n})();\nEOF\necho \"Saved engine.js\"\n OUTPUT Saved engine.js\n\n\t\t\t\t101 text Now let me plan and test the solution:\n\t\t\t\t102 button (expanded) Description: Collapse step, Secondary Actions: Collapse\n\t\t\t\t103 text Plan the solution by analyzing delivery paths\n\t\t\t\t168 text sandbox\n\t\t\t\t169 container\n\t\t\t\t\t170 text exit:  0\n\t\t\t\t\t171 container Sandbox view\n\t\t\t\t\t\t172 checkbox Description: Terminal View, Value: 1\n\t\t\t\t\t\t173 checkbox Description: Code View, Value: 0\n\t\t\t\t174 container\n\t\t\t\t\t175 text COMMAND $ cd /tmp && python3 << 'EOF'\nimport json\n\n# Puzzle analysis\npuzzle = json.loads(open('level.json').read())\n\n# Plan:\n# 1. 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? 1, y) || isWall(x + 1, y)) && (isWall(x, y - 1) || isWall(x, y + 1))));\n}\n  return { directionDelta, validateLevel, initialState, step, isWon, serialize, hash, plan, validatePlan, replay, cornerDeadlocks };\n})();\nEOF\n OUTPUT\n\t\t\t\t\t\t129 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t130 editor (settable) Editor content\n\t\t\t\t\t131 button (expanded) Description: Collapse step, Secondary Actions: Collapse\n\t\t\t\t\t132 text Create the Python adapter for the canonical simulator\n\t\t\t\t\t168 text sandbox\n\t\t\t\t\t169 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t170 text exit:  0\n\t\t\t\t\t\t171 container Sandbox view\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t172 checkbox Description: Terminal View, Value: 1\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t173 checkbox Description: Code View, Value: 0\n\t\t\t\t\t174 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t175 text COMMAND $ cat > /tmp/adapter.py << 'EOF'\n\"\"\"Execute the canonical generated JS in a Python-only TrueForge sandbox.\n\nInstall quickjs in the sandbox, then pass engine.browser.js, level.json, plan.json.\nThis adapter contains no movement rules. 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? [...beyond] : [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes + 1 };\n}\nfunction isWon(level, state) { return state.parcels.every((parcel) => level.goals.some((goal) => same(parcel, goal))); }\nfunction serialize(state) { return JSON.stringify({ duck: state.duck, parcels: [...state.parcels].sort((a, b) => key(a).localeCompare(key(b))), moves: state.moves, pushes: state.pushes }); }\nfunction hash(state) { return serialize(state); }\nfunction plan(moves) { validatePlan(moves); return { moves: [...moves] }; }\nfunction validatePlan(moves) {\n    if (!Array.isArray(moves) || moves.length > 100 || moves.some((move) => typeof move !== \"string\" || !DIRECTIONS.has(move)))\n        throw new Error(\"plan must contain at most 100 valid directions\");\n}\nfunction replay(level, moves) {\n    validatePlan(moves);\n    let state = initialState(level);\n    const trace = [serialize(state)];\n    for (const direction of moves) {\n        state = step(level, state, direction);\n        trace.push(serialize(state));\n    }\n    return { trace: Object.freeze([...trace]), finalState: state, attempts: moves.length, won: isWon(level, state) };\n}\nfunction cornerDeadlocks(level, state) {\n    const goalKeys = new Set(level.goals.map(key));\n    const width = level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isWall = (x, y) => y < 0 || y >= level.terrain.length || x < 0 || x >= width || level.terrain[y][x] === \"#\";\n    return state.parcels.filter(([x, y]) => !goalKeys.has(key([x, y])) && ((isWall(x - 1, y) || isWall(x + 1, y)) && (isWall(x, y - 1) || isWall(x, y + 1))));\n}\n  return { directionDelta, validateLevel, initialState, step, isWon, serialize, hash, plan, validatePlan, replay, cornerDeadlocks };\n})();\nEOF\ncat /tmp/engine.js | head -c 100\n OUTPUT /* Generated from sokoban/engine.ts; 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? 0;\n    if (!level.terrain.length || !width || level.terrain.some((row) => row.length !== width))\n        throw new Error(\"terrain must be a nonempty rectangle\");\n    if (!level.parcels.length || level.parcels.length !== level.goals.length)\n        throw new Error(\"parcel and goal counts must be nonempty and equal\");\n    const inBounds = (p) => Number.isInteger(p[0]) && Number.isInteger(p[1]) && p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < width;\n    const floor = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (![level.duck, ...level.parcels, ...level.goals].every(floor))\n        throw new Error(\"actors and goals must be on floor\");\n    const actors = [level.duck, ...level.parcels];\n    if (actors.some((p, index) => actors.some((other, otherIndex) => index !== otherIndex && same(p, other))))\n        throw new Error(\"actors cannot overlap\");\n}\nfunction initialState(level) { validateLevel(level); return { duck: [...level.duck], parcels: level.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: 0, pushes: 0 }; }\nfunction step(level, state, direction) {\n    const [dx, dy] = directionDelta[direction];\n    const next = [state.duck[0] + dx, state.duck[1] + dy];\n    const inBounds = (p) => p[1] >= 0 && p[1] < level.terrain.length && p[0] >= 0 && p[0] < level.terrain[0].length;\n    const isOpen = (p) => inBounds(p) && level.terrain[p[1]][p[0]] !== \"#\";\n    if (!isOpen(next))\n        return state;\n    const parcelIndex = occupied(state.parcels, next);\n    if (parcelIndex < 0)\n        return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p) => [...p]), moves: state.moves + 1, pushes: state.pushes };\n    const beyond = [next[0] + dx, next[1] + dy];\n    if (!inBounds(beyond) || !isOpen(beyond) || occupied(state.parcels, beyond) >= 0)\n        return state;\n    return { duck: [...next], parcels: state.parcels.map((p, i) => i === parcelIndex ? 1, y) || isWall(x + 1, y)) && (isWall(x, y - 1) || isWall(x, y + 1))));\n}\n  return { directionDelta, validateLevel, initialState, step, isWon, serialize, hash, plan, validatePlan, replay, cornerDeadlocks };\n})();\nEOF\necho \"Engine saved\"\n OUTPUT Engine saved\n\n\t\t\t\t94 button (expanded) Description: Collapse step, Secondary Actions: Collapse\n\t\t\t\t95 text Save the puzzle as JSON and create the Python adapter\n\t\t\t\t148 text sandbox\n\t\t\t\t149 container\n\t\t\t\t\t150 text exit:  0\n\t\t\t\t\t151 container Sandbox view\n\t\t\t\t\t\t152 checkbox Description: Terminal View, Value: 1\n\t\t\t\t\t\t153 checkbox Description: Code View, Value: 0\n\t\t\t\t154 container\n\t\t\t\t\t155 text COMMAND $ cat > /tmp/puzzle.json << 'EOF'\n{\"name\":\"Benchmark 04\",\"terrain\":[\"#########\",\"#       #\",\"#  #    #\",\"#       #\",\"#    #  #\",\"#       #\",\"#########\"],\"duck\":[4,5],\"parcels\":[[4,4],[5,2]],\"goals\":[[2,1],[6,5]]}\nEOF\n\ncat > /tmp/adapter.py << 'ADAPTER_EOF'\n\"\"\"Execute the canonical generated JS in a Python-only TrueForge sandbox.\n\nInstall quickjs in the sandbox, then pass engine.browser.js, level.json, plan.json.\nThis adapter contains no movement rules.
